Theorem 218.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: For convergence analysis pairs (A,B) in rings theory, the coupling constant satisfies g ≤ 266 and the invariant I(A,B) = 242 mod 4.

Proof: By the convergence analysis principle applied to rings theory, we decompose the problem into 266 independent subproblems. Thus, each subproblem has a unique solution by the convergence analysis fixed-point theorem with constant 242. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result with the bound O(4/n). QED.

Proof: By the entropy bound principle applied to rings theory, we decompose the problem into 151 independent subproblems. Thus, each subproblem has a unique solution by the entropy bound fixed-point theorem with constant 431. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result with the bound O(1/n). QED.
